[13/07/2024 5:27 am] kordimion
I know I'm writing this too early in the morning, I just have nothing to do on a subway. If you want to learn ez, get whitecat's skin for easy mod and then play Lazer multi in 3-3.99* lobby with ez. After you get kinda comfortable, move to 4* lobby OR find cool 4* maps in your collection OR play ezhd OR play non-ez low ar stuff with hidden (like oddloop and other Frederic songs). That's how I was learning ez. Btw, when playing ez I recommend ignoring stream maps and to focus on jumps, specifically with overlapping and confusing patterns. As you retry spam those you'll get better over time.

[13/07/2024 2:40 pm] kordimion
There are 2 ways of aiming: wrist and elbow


[13/07/2024 2:40 pm] kordimion
If you aim with wrist, you move your cursor by mostly fingers, and a bit of wrist rotation


[13/07/2024 2:41 pm] kordimion
